3.12
) are equivalent to the imposition of the
N/N^{\alpha}
rate penalty in the homogeneously weak loadings case, where its heterogeneous equivalent is
N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}
. Clearly,
\lambda_{\mathrm{max}}(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2})=N/N^{\alpha_{r}}
, so out of
r
penalties, the weakest loading weights the most. Overall,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}=(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+t^{-1}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+t^{-1}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}))\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=N^{-\alpha_{r}}\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}+t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}+t^{-1}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}+t^{-1}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}))\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right)(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}(N^{\alpha_{r}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2})

(3.14)

In what remains, we will use the same definitions of the scalar quantities in (
3.4
) - (
3.7
). However, we replace
\alpha
with
\alpha_{r}
(in the interim, absence of a subscript
\alpha
(
\alpha_{r}
) means that there is no scaling in terms of
N
). Next, to accommodate heterogeneity in loading weakness, we re-define

\displaystyle\eta_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D}=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{f}_{l})^{\prime}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,s}=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\eta_{l,s}^{D},

(3.15)

\displaystyle\nu_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D}=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{f}_{s})^{\prime}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,l}=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\nu_{l,s}^{D}.

(3.16)

Additionally, we introduce
\mathbf{Q}_{N,\alpha_{r}}=\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{r}}}
, which is
O(1)
. Then, by again using the commutative product, we get

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{s}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\gamma_{l,s}+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,s}+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,s}+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\nu_{l,s}\right)

\displaystyle=(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\mathbf{Q}^{2}_{N,\alpha_{r}}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\gamma_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D}+\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\nu_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D}\right).

(3.17)

3.2
Lemmas for PCA Convergence Rates

3.2.1
Boundedness of Eigenvalues and Rotation Matrices

Lemma 5

Under Assumptions A.1 - A.4, one has

1.

if
\alpha\in(0,1])
(homogeneous)

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\lVert\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\right\rVert^{q}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\right\|^{q}=O_{p}(1),\quad\text{for any}\;q>0.

2.

Furthermore, under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 and heterogeneous loadings (
1\geq\alpha_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\alpha_{r}>1/2
), one has

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\lVert\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\right\rVert^{q}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{q}=O_{p}(1),\quad\text{for any}\;q>0.

Proof.

\mathrm{(a)}
To demonstrate
\mathrm{(i.)}
, we note that

\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}
is the squared matrix of the
r
largest singular values –and therefore contains the
r
largest eigenvalues– of
(Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}
, for
t=k_{0},\ldots,T-1
. The term
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}
is the rate penalty term, which takes into account the fact that we normalize by
N

in practice
, while the weakness of the loadings requires
N^{\alpha}
, instead, which is unknown. Hence,
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}
is the matrix with the
r
largest eigenvalues of
(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}
(see Lemma 1 in
Bai & Ng,, 2023
), which means that we can easily convert

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\lambda_{j}\left((Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=\lambda_{j}\left((N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\right).

(3.18)

Without loss of generality we assume the
original
eigenvalues in
\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}
are arranged in non-decreasing order as:
d_{1,t}^{2}\geq\cdots\geq d_{r,t}^{2}
. It follows that
\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}=\frac{N^{\alpha}}{N}\times\operatorname{diag}\left(d_{j,t}^{-2}\right)
for
d_{1,t}^{-2}\leq\cdots\leq d_{r,t}^{-2}
, and

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}=\operatorname{tr}\left(N^{-2(1-\alpha)}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{-4}\right)=N^{-2(1-\alpha)}\sum_{j=1}^{r}d_{j,t}^{-4}\leq N^{-2(1-\alpha)}rd_{r,t}^{-4}.

(3.19)

In what follows, it is enough to show boundedness of power 1, because squaring will not change the outcome. By taking supremum over
t
then

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\leq r^{1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|N^{(\alpha-1)}d_{r,t}^{-2}|=N^{(\alpha-1)}r^{1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{-2}|=r^{1/2}\frac{N^{(\alpha-1)}}{\inf_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{2}|}.

(3.20)

Therefore, to show (
i.
) it is sufficient to show that
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\inf_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{2}|=O_{p}(1)
, or, alternatively, that the leading component of
\inf_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{2}|
is
O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha}}{N}\right)
. For that, we use a similar strategy to Lemma A.5 in
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
. For
t=k_{0},\ldots,T-1
,
s=1,\ldots,t
then we let
\mathbf{x}_{s}=\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{f}_{s}+\mathbf{e}_{s}
as the horizontal slice of
\mathbf{X}_{t}
, where
\mathbf{x}_{s},\mathbf{e}_{s}\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
,
\mathbf{f}_{s}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
,
\mathbf{\Lambda}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times r}
. The scaled population covariance of
\mathbf{x}_{s}
is then achieved by the conditional law of iterated expectations

\displaystyle N^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}

\displaystyle=N^{(\alpha-1)}N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{x}_{s}\mathbf{x}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})

\displaystyle=N^{(\alpha-1)}\left[N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{e}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{e}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\right]

\displaystyle=N^{(\alpha-1)}\Big[N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda},\mathbf{F}_{t})|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{e}_{s}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda},\mathbf{F}_{t})\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})

\displaystyle+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{e}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda},\mathbf{F}_{t})|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\Big]

\displaystyle=N^{(\alpha-1)}\left[N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime})\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}+N^{-\alpha}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\right]

\displaystyle=N^{(\alpha-1)}\left[N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}+N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}\right],

(3.21)

where we used that the cross-product in expectation between
\mathbf{f}_{s}
and
\mathbf{e}_{s}
is zero by Assumption A.2,i) and the usual PC factors normalization condition
\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}|\boldsymbol{\Lambda})=\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime})=\mathbf{I}_{r}
due to independence between the loadings and the factors, implying orthogonality of the factors. Now, by Assumption A.3,iii) we know that the
r\times r
matrix
N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}>0
and diagonal, meaning its eigenvalues are all distinct and positive. The non-zero eigenvalues of
\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}
and
\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}
are necessarily the same
19
19

19

This is a standard result: by SVD of
\mathbf{\Lambda}=\mathbf{U}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{V}^{\prime}
, then
\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}=\mathbf{V}\mathbf{S}^{\prime}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{V}^{\prime}
and
\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}=\mathbf{U}\mathbf{S}\mathbf{S}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}^{\prime}
, where:
\mathbf{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times r}
is diagonal with eigenvalues of
\mathbf{\Lambda}
,
\mathbf{U}\in\mathbb{R}^{N\times N}
is an orthogonal matrix of left singular vectors,
\mathbf{V}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
is an orthogonal matrix of right singular vectors. It follows that the eigenvalues in
\mathbf{S}\mathbf{S}^{\prime}
and
\mathbf{S}^{\prime}\mathbf{S}
are the same, and the same of
\mathbf{\Lambda}.
and the remaining
N-r
eigenvalues of
\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}
are zero. Then, the idea --by Weil’s theorem
20
20

20

For any two Hermitian matrices
\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
, and their sum
\mathbf{C}=\mathbf{A}+\mathbf{B}
, then for
j,i=1:n
it holds that
\lambda_{j+i-1}(\mathbf{C})\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A})+\lambda_{i}(\mathbf{B})
, for
j+i\leq n+1
. In the present case
\mathbf{C}:=N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma},\;\mathbf{A}:=N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda},\;\mathbf{B}:=N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}
and
i=\max=1.

– is to bound the absolute difference between the
j
th eigenvalue of
N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}
and the
j
th eigenvalue of
N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}
,
j=1,\ldots,r
, by the max eigenvalue of
N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}
, which is bounded (away from infinity) by Assumption A.2,v). Namely, for any
\alpha\in(0,1]

\displaystyle\left|\lambda_{j}(N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma})-\lambda_{j}(N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda})\right|\leq N^{-\alpha}\lambda_{\max}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}\right)=O(N^{-\alpha})=o(1),

(3.22)

where we used the notation
\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A})
(
\lambda_{\max}(\mathbf{A})
) the
j
th(
\max
) eigenvalue of any matrix
\mathbf{A}
, such that e.g.,
d_{j,t}^{2}=\lambda_{j}((Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime})=N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{j}((N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime})
and
d_{r,t}^{2}=\lambda_{\max}((Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime})=N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{\max}((N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime})
. Then, Weyl’s theorem again (see footnote 3), and using as above the fact that
(Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}
and
(Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}
share the same non-zero eigenvalues and we can easily convert between the eigenvalues of
(Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}
and
(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}
, yields

\displaystyle\left|d_{j,t}^{2}-N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{j}(N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|(Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-1}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=N^{(\alpha-1)}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|,

(3.23)

from which by Assumption A.3,iii)

\displaystyle d_{r,t}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{r}(N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda})-N^{(\alpha-1)}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}

(3.24)

\displaystyle\geq N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}})/2-N^{(\alpha-1)}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}.

(3.25)

Then, taking infimum wrt
t
, we get

\displaystyle\inf_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{2}|\geq N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}})/2-N^{(\alpha-1)}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}

(3.26)

As
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}
is positive definite as of Assumption A.3,iii), we can treat
N^{(\alpha-1)}\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}})/2=O\left(\frac{N^{\alpha}}{N}\right)
as the dominant component of
\inf_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{2}|
, which is what we were supposed to achieve in the light of (
3.20
). What remains to be shown to prove (i.) is that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}=o_{p}(1).
Expanding
N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}
using (
3.21
), and given that by the factor model decomposition one has

\displaystyle(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}=N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}\left(t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}+N^{-\alpha}\left(t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{e}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}\right)+N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}+N^{-\alpha}t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{e}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime},

(3.27)

thus

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|G_{1,t}\|+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|G_{2,t}\|+2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|G_{3,t}\|

(3.28)

for
G_{1,t}=N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}\left(t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}
, using again the PC factors normalization condition (Assumption A3.2);
G_{2,t}=N^{-\alpha}\left(t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{e}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}-\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}\right)
;
G_{3,t}=N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Lambda}t^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime},
and using triangle inequality coupled with the fact that
\|\mathbf{A}\|_{sp}\leq\|\mathbf{A}\|
for any matrix
\mathbf{A}.
We now proceed to show each of these
\sup
-terms is
o_{p}(1)
. Starting with
G_{1,t}
, by factoring out of the
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}
the scaled loadings, which do not depend on
t
, one has

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|G_{1,t}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{\mathbf{\Lambda}}{N^{\alpha/2}}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{T}{t}\right)\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\right\|=O_{p}(1/\sqrt{T})=o_{p}(1),

which follows by the fact that
\left\|\frac{\mathbf{\Lambda}}{N^{\alpha/2}}\right\|^{2}=\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}/N^{\alpha}\right)=O(1)
by Assumption A3.3, the fact that
T/k_{0}=O(1)
since
k_{0}=\lfloor T\pi_{0}\rfloor
for
\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
, and by Assumption A3,ii). Now onto
G_{2,t}
.
By union bound, for any
\varepsilon>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|G_{2,t}\|>\varepsilon\right)=\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\{\|G_{2,t}\|>\varepsilon\}\right)\leq\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\mathbb{P}\left(\|G_{2,t}\|>\varepsilon\right)\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\|G_{2,t}\|^{2}\right),

where the last inequality follows from squaring and applying Markov’s inequality. So, we are onto showing that
\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\|G_{2,t}\|^{2}=o(1).
Now, using that for any square-
N
matrix
\mathbf{A}
, the Frobenius norm gives
\|\mathbf{A}\|^{2}=\operatorname{tr}(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{A})=\sum_{i,j=1}^{N}a_{i,j}^{2}
, then for
t=k_{0},\ldots,T-1

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\|G_{2,t}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N^{2\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}(e_{i,s}e_{j,s}-\mathbb{E}(e_{i,s}e_{j,s}))\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{1}{t^{2}}\sum_{s_{1}=1}^{t}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{t}\mathrm{C}ov\left(e_{i,s_{1}}e_{j,s_{1}},e_{i,s_{2}}e_{j,s_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T}{k_{0}^{2}}\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{T}\left|\mathrm{C}ov\left(e_{i,s_{1}}e_{j,s_{1}},e_{i,s_{2}}e_{j,s_{2}}\right)\right|\right),

from which it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\|G_{2,t}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\|G_{2,t}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{T}{k_{0}}\right)^{2}\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{T}\left|\mathrm{C}ov\left(e_{i,s_{1}}e_{j,s_{1}},e_{i,s_{2}}e_{j,s_{2}}\right)\right|\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\right)=o(1),

by Assumption A2.iv) and for
\alpha>1/2
, which implies that overall

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|G_{2,t}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha}}\right).

(3.29)

Finally, for
G_{3,t}
we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|G_{3,t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{N^{\alpha/2}}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha/2}}\mathbf{\Lambda}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\left\|\frac{\mathbf{\Lambda}}{N^{\alpha/2}}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{e}_{s}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}e_{i,s}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{k_{0}}\left(\frac{T}{k_{0}}\right)\frac{C}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}e_{i,s}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{C}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}e_{i,s}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{k_{0}}\left(\frac{T}{k_{0}}\right)C\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}e_{i,s}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)=o_{p}(1),

provided that the rate restriction is satisfied, where the latter inequalities follow from the fact that
\left\|\frac{\mathbf{\Lambda}}{N^{\alpha/2}}\right\|^{2}=\operatorname{tr}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}/N^{\alpha}\right)=O(1)
by Assumption A3.3, the fact that
T/k_{0}=O(1)
since
k_{0}=\lfloor T\pi_{0}\rfloor
for
\pi_{0}\in(0,1)
, and by Assumption A.3,v). Hence,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|G_{3,t}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right).

(3.30)

Finally, to put the results together,

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\inf_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|d_{r,t}^{2}|

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}})/2-\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N^{\alpha}t)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}-N^{-\alpha}\mathbf{\Sigma}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}})/2+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)

(3.31)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\leq\frac{2r^{1/2}}{\lambda_{\min}(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}})}+o_{p}(1).

(3.32)

This proves (i.).

Now onto
\mathrm{(ii.)}
. As in
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
we show the result for
q=2
, since
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|^{q}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left(\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|^{2}\right)^{q/2}=\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|^{2}\right)^{q/2}=O_{p}(1)
if
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|^{2}=O_{p}(1)
, which holds for any
q>0.
Hence, for
t=k_{0},\ldots,T-1
by submultiplicativity of the Frobenius norm we get

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|^{2}\leq\underbrace{\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}}_{(I)}\underbrace{\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|^{2}}_{(II)}\underbrace{\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|^{2}}_{(III)}.

The term
(I)
is
O_{p}(1)
, as proved in (i.) of part (a), while
(III)=O(1)
by Assumption A.3,iii). Therefore, it is sufficient to prove the term
(II)=O_{p}(1)
. Note how

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|^{2}\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right)\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=r\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\mathrm{tr}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)=O_{p}(1).

(3.33)

Now we turn to the heterogeneous loading cases in part
(\mathrm{b})
. To begin with
\mathrm{(i.)}
, we will follow a different approach since in the heterogeneous case we have different convergence rates for the loadings as given by
\mathbf{B}_{N}
, which need to be taken into account in the eigenvalue analysis. The strategy in
\mathrm{(i.)}
of part (a) crucially hinges on the homogeneous rate. Therefore, we will start with the slightly modified expansion as in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{Nt}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+N^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+N^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+N^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}+N^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle+N^{-1}t^{-1}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right).

(3.34)

By following the discussion in the proof of Lemma 1 in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
, if the four last terms on the right-hand side are negligible, then the eigenvalue matrix of
\frac{1}{Nt}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}
, represented by
\mathbf{D}^{2}_{Nt,r}
, will be determined by the eigenvalues of
N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}=N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}
. Note that the later asymtotically has the same eigenvalues as

\displaystyle N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)N^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{2}_{N}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)N^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{2}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(3.35)

because
\mathbf{B}_{N}
and
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}
are diagonal, and

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)N^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|\left\|N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(3.36)

This implies that

\displaystyle(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2})N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}

\displaystyle=(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2})\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)N^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{2}_{N}

\displaystyle+(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2})\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)N^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(3.37)

due to

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2})\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)N^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|\left\|N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\times\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(3.38)

Recall that
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}
has positive and distinct eigenvalues by assumption, which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{j}}}\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{Nt}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{j}}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r,jj}^{2}=\lambda_{j}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)+o_{p}(1).

(3.39)

Lastly, since
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}
additionally is diagonal, we obtain that
N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}=\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)
. Therefore, by the CMT

\displaystyle\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}=\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)^{-1}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(3.40)

which exists by assumption. Hence, to demonstrate that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
, we need to check that the orders of the remainder terms hold in supremum sense, because
\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)^{-1}
is independent of
t
. We already have (
3.36
) and (
3.38
), where the latter used the rate correction
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}
. Therefore, by using
\left\|\mathbf{A}\right\|_{sp}\leq\left\|\mathbf{A}\right\|
and the rate correction, we continue with

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}N^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|_{sp}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|_{sp}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}N^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|_{sp}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|_{sp}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\frac{T}{t}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}T^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\left\|N^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|N^{-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{k_{0}}}T^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{1/2+\alpha_{1}/2}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{\alpha_{1}/2-\alpha_{r}}\right),

(3.41)

since
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}=\max\{\sqrt{N},\sqrt{T}\}
. Also, because the trace gives a finite sum,

\displaystyle\left\|N^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\left(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right)}=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{r}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lambda_{i,j}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{r}\frac{N^{\alpha_{j}}}{N}\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha_{j}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lambda_{i,j}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{r}\sup_{\alpha_{r}\leq j\leq\alpha_{1}}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{j}}}{N}\right)\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha_{j}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lambda_{i,j}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{r}\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha_{j}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\lambda_{i,j}^{2}\right)}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}}\right)

(3.42)

even under heterogeneous
\alpha
, because
\alpha_{1}\leq 1
. While its limit is positive definite only under strong loadings, it is sufficient for boundedness.
21
21

21

Alternatively, by avoiding the high-level condition in
A.3
vii),

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\left\|N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right)=o_{p}(1)

if we additionally assume that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
(see (9) in
Bai & Ng,, 2023
), which is a slightly worse rate.
The upcoming term is just a transpose of the latter, and the analysis is identical. Next,

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}T^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\left(N^{-2}T^{-2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(e_{i,s}^{2})\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}(e_{i,s}^{2})\right)^{2}}=O\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)

(3.43)

and

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}t^{-1}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right\|_{sp}\leq\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}T^{-1}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\left[N^{-2}T^{-2}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{\prime}\right]}

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}(e_{i,s}^{2}-\mathbb{E}(e_{i,s}^{2}))\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{TN}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(e_{i,s}^{2}-\mathbb{E}(e_{i,s}^{2}))\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{TN}}\right).

(3.44)

This completes the proof.

Remark 6

Note that the statement of Lemma
5
is formulated in terms of Frobenius norm, but the remainder rate in (
3.41
) is based on the spectral norm. However, in this case they are equivalent, because we can use
\left\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}\right\|\leq\left\|\mathbf{A}\right\|_{sp}\left\|\mathbf{B}\right\|
(see Lemma A.1 in
Bai & Ng,, 2023
). Particularly,

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|N^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\left\|N^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|N^{-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{T}{k_{0}}}T^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{1/2+\alpha_{1}/2}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{\alpha_{1}/2-\alpha_{r}}\right).

Therefore, we obtain the same result.

Finally, to show
\mathrm{(ii.)}
it is sufficient to observe how by submultiplicativity of the Frobenius norm

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1),

because the component
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}
is bounded in probability for
t=T
as argued in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
, and it can be shown to be bounded in probability in supremum sense. In particular, note that by using eigenvector-eigenvalue relationship and the fact that
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=t\mathbf{I}_{r}
, we get

\displaystyle N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}^{2}_{Nt,r}=\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1},

(3.45)

where

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|_{sp}^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|_{sp}\left\|N^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\left\|N^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sqrt{\frac{T}{k_{0}}}T^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}r\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\left\|N^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|\times\sqrt{\frac{T}{k_{0}}}T^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}}\right)\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{1/2-\alpha_{r}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{1/2+\alpha_{1}/2}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{\alpha_{1}/2-\alpha_{r}}\right),

(3.46)

where the next term is just a transpose, so its order is the same.
22
22

22

Alternatively, by avoiding the high-level condition in
A.3
vii),

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}r\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|_{sp}\left\|N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha_{r})/2}).

Next,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{t^{2}}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-\alpha_{r}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|_{sp}^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|^{2}_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq N^{-\alpha_{r}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|^{2}\frac{T}{k_{0}}T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|^{2}_{sp}

\displaystyle=N^{-\alpha_{r}}\left\|N^{\alpha_{r}/2}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{2}r\frac{T}{k_{0}}T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|^{2}_{sp}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}}).

(3.47)

Therefore, by combining this analysis with (
3.40
), we have that

\displaystyle N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}^{2}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}=\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+o_{p}(1),

(3.48)

where the remainder is negligible uniformly in
t
. Because
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}
is bounded for all
t
, it must be that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
, as well. Note that (
3.48
) implies that since
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}
is invertible asymptotically,
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}
is invertble and has an invertible probability limit, as well (see also a discussion on p. 1903 in
Bai & Ng,, 2023
). Consequently,
\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}^{-1}
exists.

3.2.2
Approximation of the Factor Space

For the further result, we observe that the statistics in
Pitarakis, (2025)
and
Pitarakis, (2023)
are comprised of the components of the form
\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}d_{u}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1},\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})
, where
\frac{\lfloor f_{j}(T)\rfloor}{T}\to q_{j}
for
j=1,2
, such that
q_{2}>q_{1}
and some loss-differential
d_{u}(.)
. We implicitly allow
\lfloor f_{j}(T)\rfloor
to subsume additive constants as they will vanish in the limit when divided by
T
. Moreover,
\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor\geq k_{0}
and
\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor\leq T-1
. For example, in case of
\varepsilon_{f}(m_{0})
in (
2.1
), the first component has
\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor=\lfloor T\pi_{0}\rfloor=k_{0}
and
\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor=T-1
with
q_{1}=\pi_{0}
and
q_{2}=1
. The second component also entails
\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor=\lfloor T\pi_{0}\rfloor=k_{0}
,
q_{1}=\pi_{0}
, but
\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor+\lfloor(T-k_{0})\mu_{0}\rfloor-1=k_{0}+m_{0}-1
and
q_{2}=\pi_{0}+(1-\pi_{0})\mu_{0}
. Similar summation limits apply to every component of every considered statistic. Therefore, it is convenient to formulate the following lemma with the bounds on
\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}d_{u}(.)
, where
d_{T}=(\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor-\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor+1)
and
d_{u}(.)
will obey expansions of the infeasible statistics.

Lemma 6

Under A.1 - A.4 as
(N,T)\to\infty
,

1.

if
\alpha\in(0,1])
(homogeneous):

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\hskip 5.69054pt\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\hskip 5.69054pt\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{s}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha});

2.

Furthermore, under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 and heterogeneous loadings (
1\geq\alpha_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\alpha_{r}>1/2
), one has

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\hskip 5.69054pt\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha_{r}}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\hskip 5.69054pt\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{s}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha_{r}}).

Proof.
(a) To demonstrate
\mathrm{(i.)}
, we begin by following
Bai & Ng, (2002)
and by using the expansion in (
3.8
) (with the time index shifted to the out-of-sample set) in connection to
(a+b+c+d)^{2}\leq 4(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2}+d^{2})
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}\times\left(\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}\right),

(3.49)

where we will analyse
\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{IV}
separately. Indeed, by Cauchy-Schwarz (CS) inequality

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}=\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\right\||\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=r\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\gamma_{l,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right),

(3.50)

where we used the fact that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}=\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\sum_{p=1}^{r}\widehat{f}_{p,l}^{2}=\sum_{p=1}^{r}\underbrace{\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{f}_{p,l}^{2}\right)}_{\text{1 by the restriction}}=r

(3.51)

and

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\right)=\left(\frac{d_{T}k_{0}}{T^{2}}\right)^{-1}=\left((q_{2}-q_{1})\pi_{0}\right)^{-1}+O(T^{-1}).

We will use these two facts for the remainder. Note that this rate is worse than the one in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
, where they have an extra
T^{-1}
. This penalty comes from the fact that the rotation matrix changes for each iteration of the recursion in the current case. To continue, by using an identical chain of inequalities, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}=\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\xi_{l,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\right)=O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}),

(3.52)

because
\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\xi_{l,t}^{2}=O_{p}(N^{-1})
by our assumptions, which implies that
\alpha>1/2
in order to make this term negligible. Note that to obtain the mean-square out-of-sample factor estimation error we already cannot allow the loadings be too weak. In comparison,
Bai & Ng, (2023)
do not need this since they do not have rate punishment coming from the recursion. Further

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}=\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha}),

(3.53)

and similarly

\displaystyle\mathbf{IV}=\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha})

(3.54)

by our assumptions.

For
\mathrm{(ii.)}
, we will apply a similar chain of arguments to create inequalities on the expansion in (
3.8
):

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{s}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}\times\left(\mathbf{i}+\mathbf{ii}+\mathbf{iii}+\mathbf{iv}\right),

(3.55)

where we will investigate
\mathbf{i}-\mathbf{iv}
in turn. To begin with,

\displaystyle\mathbf{i}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\gamma_{l,s,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\right\||\gamma_{l,s,\alpha}|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\gamma_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=r\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\gamma_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{k_{0}^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\gamma_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\frac{1}{k_{0}}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\gamma_{l,s}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right),

(3.56)

where we used the fact that
\frac{T}{k_{0}}=\left(\frac{k_{0}}{T}\right)^{-1}=\pi_{0}^{-1}+O(T^{-1})
. The rest of the terms follow a similar fashion. In particular,

\displaystyle\mathbf{ii}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,s,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\xi_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{k_{0}^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\xi_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{T^{2}}{k_{0}^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\xi_{l,s}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\right)=O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}),

(3.57)

\displaystyle\mathbf{iii}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,s,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\eta_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{k_{0}^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\eta_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{T^{2}}{k_{0}^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\eta_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha})

(3.58)

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{iv}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\nu_{l,s,\alpha}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\nu_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{k_{0}^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\nu_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=r\left(\frac{T^{2}}{k_{0}^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}\nu_{l,s,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha}),

(3.59)

which completes
(\mathrm{ii})
.

(b) Both
(\mathrm{i.})-(\mathrm{ii.})
are very similar to part (a), as we only need the heterogeneity-adjusted expansion in (
3.17
) for both cases. Clearly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{Q}^{2}_{N,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\times\left(\mathbf{I}+\mathbf{II}+\mathbf{III}+\mathbf{IV}\right),

(3.60)

where each
\mathbf{I}
-
\mathbf{IV}
follows the same chain of arguments as the respective terms in part (
\mathrm{i.}
) of part (a). Instead of
N/N^{\alpha}
we will have
N/N^{\alpha_{r}}
penalizing the rates, which coincides with the largest eigenvalue of
N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}
, meaning that the weakest loading is the dominant one in determining the rates. The rate is slower for
\mathbf{III}
and
\mathbf{IV},
, however. For instance,

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}=\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha_{r}}^{D}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\eta_{l,t,\alpha_{r}}^{D})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}(\eta_{l,t}^{D})^{2}

\displaystyle=r\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\left(\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}(\eta_{l,t}^{D})^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\right)

(3.61)

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{iii}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\eta_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D})^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\frac{1}{k_{0}^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}(\eta_{l,s,\alpha_{r}}^{D})^{2}

\displaystyle=r\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\left(\frac{T^{2}}{k_{0}^{2}}\right)\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T-1}\sum_{l=1}^{T-1}(\eta_{l,s}^{D})^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\right)

(3.62)

and the same for
\mathbf{IV}
(
\mathbf{iv}
). For the rest, we use A.3 iv) for the heterogeneous case. Part (
\mathrm{ii.}
) follows the same discussion. Note that the change from
\alpha
to
\alpha_{r}
is the same as in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
, while other changes occur due to the recursive setup.

3.2.3
Limits of Eigenvalues and Rotation Matrices

Lemma 7

Under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 as
(N,T)\to\infty

1.

If
\alpha\in(0,1])
(homogeneous):

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}).

2.

Furthermore, under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 and heterogeneous loadings (
1\geq\alpha_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\alpha_{r}>1/2
), one has

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|=O\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{r}}}}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{[(1+\alpha_{1})-3\alpha_{r}]/2}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha_{r})/2}).

Proof
.
\mathrm{(a)}
To see
\mathrm{(i.)}
, we base the approach on Lemma 2 in
Bai & Ng, (2002)
, and decomposition
t^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}=\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}+\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}
. We then obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-t^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}),

(3.63)

uniformly in
t
, since

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(3.64)

Next,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\|\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{-2}(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1)

(3.65)

as was demonstrated in Lemma 1
\mathrm{(a)}
part
\mathrm{(ii.)}
. Moreover,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sqrt{r}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}),

(3.66)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}).

(3.67)

This implies that
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{H}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})
uniformly in
t
, where
\mathbf{H}_{r}
is an orthogonal matrix with only
\pm 1
on the main diagonal. Then

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}).

(3.68)

For
\mathrm{(ii.)}
, we will now use the argument based on the equations (20) - (24) of
Bai & Ng, (2013)
to firstly demonstrate that
\mathbf{H}_{r}
is also diagonal only. To begin with, we know that

\displaystyle t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=t^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}+\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})

(3.69)

uniformly in
t
, based on the same bounds used above. Now, by the definition of
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}
and by using (
3.69
) we get

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}),

(3.70)

and hence uniformly in
t
,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}),

(3.71)

and by using
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{H}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})
,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)\mathbf{H}_{r}=\mathbf{H}_{r}(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}).

(3.72)

This chain of arguments tells us that up to a negligible term,
\mathbf{H}_{r}
is a matrix consisting of eigenvectors of
N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}
, where the latter is diagonal and by assumption has all distinct eigenvalues. This implies that each eigenvalue is associated with a unique unitary eigenvector up to the sign and each of the eigenvectors has a single non-zero element (see the discussion on p. 27 in
Bai & Ng,, 2013
). Therefore,
\mathbf{H}_{r}
is a diagonal matrix with eigenvalues of
\pm 1
up to the remainder. This again verifies that
\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}
exists.
Therefore,
\mathrm{(ii.)}
comes as an important implication of (
3.72
), because it leads to

\displaystyle\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}),

(3.73)

uniformly in
t
, because
O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})
dominates
O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})
, and so

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2}).

(3.74)

\mathrm{(b)}
To demonstrate
\mathrm{(i.)}
, we can invoke exactly the same decomposition as in
(\mathrm{i.})
of part (a) with an important modification, to obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})^{\prime}-t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})^{\prime}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{r}}}}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{[(1+\alpha_{1})-3\alpha_{r}]/2}\right),

(3.75)

because

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(3.76)

which is clearly dominated by
O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)
.
Moreover,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha_{r})/2})\right)\times O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{r}}}}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{[(1+\alpha_{1})-3\alpha_{r}]/2}\right),

(3.77)

which clearly dominates the rate of
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|
. The order comes from

\displaystyle\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\times\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}}=\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{k_{0}^{1/4}}\times\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}}\frac{1}{k_{0}^{1/4}}

\displaystyle=O(1)\times\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{k_{0}^{1/4}}\times\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}\times\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{r}}}{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}

\displaystyle=O(1)\times\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}\times\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{r}}}{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}=O\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{r}}}}{N}\right)

\displaystyle=o(1)

(3.78)

under
A.4
and because
\alpha_{1}\leq 1
. Additionally,
3\alpha_{r}>1+\alpha_{1}\Longleftrightarrow\alpha_{1}<3\alpha_{r}-1
for
\alpha_{r}>0.5
. Then

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|=O\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{r}}}}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(N^{[(1+\alpha_{1})-3\alpha_{r}]/2}\right).

(3.79)

Note that the rate can be improved by employing high-level conditions, as discussed in (
3.80
) and (
3.82
).

For
\mathrm{(ii.)}
, we can use different strategies, but it is convenient to employ the conclusion in (
3.40
) directly in combination with
\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)^{-1}=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2})
by the CMT.

3.2.4
Extra Results: Better Rates for In-Sample Factor Approximation and Eigenvalues

Note that in order to prove part
\mathrm{(i.)}
of
\mathrm{(a)}
and
\mathrm{(b)}
of Lemma 2, we need to rely on the strategy of
Bai & Ng, (2002)
. In particular, we average over the out-of-sample expansions in (
3.8
) and (
3.17
), which allows to track the behavior of the rotation matrix, which changes for every
t=k_{0},\ldots,T-1
. For the in-sample analysis (parts
\mathrm{(ii.)}
), we can utilize a high-level condition in
A.3
viii) similarly to
Bai & Ng, (2023)
and improve the rate of convergence in both directions of
N
and
T
.

Lemma 8

Under A.1 - A.4 as
(N,T)\to\infty
,

1.

if
\alpha\in(0,1])
(homogeneous):

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{s}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha});

2.

Furthermore, under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 and heterogeneous loadings (
1\geq\alpha_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\alpha_{r}>1/2
), one has

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}_{Nt,r}\right\|^{2}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{s}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}}).

Proof.

\mathrm{(a)}
. In homogeneous case, note that by using
\|\mathbf{A}\|_{sp}\leq\left\|\mathbf{A}\right\|
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}t^{3/2}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}+\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}t^{3/2}}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}+\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}t^{3/2}}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right)\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}t^{3/2}}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|_{sp}+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}t^{3/2}}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|_{sp}\right)

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})\times 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}N^{-\alpha/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sqrt{r}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{r}\frac{T}{k_{0}}N^{-\alpha}T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}),

(3.80)

since
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}N^{-\alpha/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
as argued in equation (9) in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
.

\mathrm{(b)}
. In the heterogeneous case, we can follow the exact same logic as in Section 5.1 in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
and redefine
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}
and
\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}
, such that still
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N}=\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}
, and it is possible to obtain faster convergence rate. In particular now,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}=(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2})^{-1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right)

(3.81)

and so

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|_{sp}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|_{sp}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t^{3/2}}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|_{sp}+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t^{3/2}}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|_{sp}\right)

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{sp}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2})\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}})\times\sqrt{r}\frac{T}{k_{0}}T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\right\|_{sp}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2}),

(3.82)

if
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{E}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
, where a similar argument is used in the proof of the statement (25) in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
.

Similarly, the rates of the eigenvalues and the rotation matrices can be improved upon.

Lemma 9

Under A.1 - A.4 as
(N,T)\to\infty

1.

If
\alpha\in(0,1])
(homogeneous):

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}).

2.

Furthermore, under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 and heterogeneous loadings (
1\geq\alpha_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\alpha_{r}>1/2
), one has

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha_{r})/2}).

Proof
.
\mathrm{(a)}
In (i.) We will use the result (i.) from Lemma
8
, where a faster convergence rate for the in-sample factor approximation is obtained. Specifically,

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-t^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}).

(3.83)

Therefore,
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{H}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})
uniformly in
t
, where
\mathbf{H}_{r}
is again an orthogonal matrix with only
\pm 1
