on the main diagonal. Ultimately,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}).

(3.84)

For (ii.), we can again use exactly the same chain of arguments, which now lead to

\displaystyle\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}),

(3.85)

and hence
\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)\mathbf{H}_{r}=\mathbf{H}_{r}(N^{-\alpha}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda})+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})
, leading to

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2}).

(3.86)

(b) In (i.), we use use the same approach

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{I}_{r}-(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})^{\prime}-t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})

\displaystyle-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})^{\prime}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right),

(3.87)

because

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right)(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(3.88)

For the final rate, it is sufficient to demonstrate the rate of the second component. In particular,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{H}}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2})\right)\times O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{r}}}{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\times\underbrace{O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\right)}_{O_{p}(1)}\times O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\right)

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right)+o_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N^{\alpha_{r}}}{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)=o_{p}(1)

(3.89)

under
\alpha_{r}>1/2
and the fact that under
A.4
and
\alpha_{1}\leq 1
, we have
O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\right)=\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N}\right)
. Hence,
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}=\mathbf{H}_{r}+O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right)
and so

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{\alpha_{1}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N^{\alpha_{1}}}}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\right).

(3.90)

For (ii.), we leave the same convergence rate. We conjecture that it can be improved, via derivation of the exact limit of
\mathbf{B}_{N}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}
.

Appendix 4
Theoretical Results II: Asymptotic Equivalence

4.1
Expansions of the Feasible Statistics

Subscript “
f
” (“
\widehat{f}
”) indicates that the test is
infeasible
(
feasible
) since we need to use the estimated factors. The
feasible
statistic of forecast encompassing is

\displaystyle g_{\widehat{f},1}=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{1,t+1}-\frac{1}{2}\left[\frac{n}{m_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}+\frac{n}{n-m_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}+m_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}\right]\right).

(4.1)

By following
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
, the latter can be conveniently decomposed into

\displaystyle g_{\widehat{f},1}

\displaystyle=g_{f,1}+g_{f,1}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{1}}{\widehat{\omega}_{1}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\widehat{\omega}_{1}}\left(\frac{n}{m_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})+\frac{n}{n-m_{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}+m_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right).

(4.2)

Similarly, the test for forecasting accuracy in (
2.2
) admits the following expansion:

\displaystyle g_{\widehat{f},2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{2}\frac{n}{l^{0}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l^{0}_{1}-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{1,t+1}-\frac{l^{0}_{1}}{l^{0}_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l^{0}_{2}-1}\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{2}\frac{n}{l^{0}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l^{0}_{1}-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{1,t+1}-\frac{l^{0}_{1}}{l^{0}_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l^{0}_{2}-1}\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}\right)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{z}\frac{n}{l^{0}_{1}}\frac{l_{1}^{0}}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=g_{f,2}+g_{f,2}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{2}}{\widehat{\omega}_{2}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{2}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=g_{f,2}+g_{f,2}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{2}}{\widehat{\omega}_{2}}-1\right)+\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{2}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{2}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}.

(4.3)

Generally, the expansion of (
2.5
) for some
j=3,4
, and
k,p=1,2
with
k\neq p
can be represented with

\displaystyle g_{\widehat{f},j}(\lambda_{k}^{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{j}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{p}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}g_{\widehat{f},2}(\lfloor n\lambda^{0}_{k}\rfloor,l_{p})

\displaystyle=g_{f,j}+g_{f,j}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{j}}{\widehat{\omega}_{j}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l_{p}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{j}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l_{p}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

(4.4)

where we use the fact that the estimation error enters only the second model component. Now, if we set
j=3
and
k=2
, which means that we fix
l^{0}=\lfloor n\lambda_{2}^{0}\rfloor
and are left with
p=1
, we obtain

\displaystyle g_{\widehat{f},3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{1}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}g_{\widehat{f},2}(\lfloor n\lambda^{0}_{2}\rfloor,l_{1})

\displaystyle=g_{f,3}+g_{f,3}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}{\widehat{\omega}_{3}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l_{1}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{l=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=g_{f,3}+g_{f,3}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}{\widehat{\omega}_{3}}-1\right)+\left(\frac{n-\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\right)\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{n-\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\right)\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=g_{f,3}+g_{f,3}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}{\widehat{\omega}_{3}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{n-\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\right)\left(\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{n}{l_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right).

(4.5)

Note that because
\frac{n-\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}{n}=1-\tau_{0}+O(n^{-1})
, we have that
\frac{n-\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}{n(1-\tau_{0})}=1+O(n^{-1})
, which means that analysis of (
4.5
) is almost identical to (
4.3
). Intuition of this is that the portion of the expansion involving the factor estimation error does not depend on
l_{1}
. Naturally, expansion of (
2.4
) is based on the fact that we fix
l_{1}^{0}=\lfloor n\lambda_{1}^{0}\rfloor
:

\displaystyle g_{\widehat{f},4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}g_{\widehat{f},2}(\lfloor n\lambda^{0}_{1}\rfloor,l_{2})

\displaystyle=g_{f,4}+g_{f,4}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\omega}_{4}}{\widehat{\omega}_{4}}-1\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\left(\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right).

(4.6)

We are left to expand
|\widetilde{\omega}^{2}_{j}-\widehat{\omega}^{2}_{j}|
for
j=1,\ldots,4
to demonstrate that such terms are negligible. A crucial step here is to expand all the components of the estimated variances in terms of the quantities that constitute (
4.3
) - (
4.5
), so that the rates would be known. Let
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j}
for
j=1,\ldots,4
be a vector of tuning parameters associated with the respective statistic. For instance
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{1}=(\mu_{0},\pi_{0})^{\prime}
. It can be shown that for a
known
positive function
f_{j}
the difference can be written as

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\omega}^{2}_{j}-\widehat{\omega}^{2}_{j}|=|f_{j}(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j})\widetilde{\phi}^{2}-f_{j}(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j})\widehat{\phi}^{2}|=f_{j}(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j})|\widetilde{\phi}^{2}-\widehat{\phi}^{2}|,

(4.7)

where
\phi^{2}=\lim_{T\to\infty}\mathbb{V}ar\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}-\mathbb{E}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}))\right]
. Finding the estimator of the later requires imposing some dependence structure. For instance,
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
and
Stauskas & Westerlund, (2022)
use a martingale difference assumption directly (their estimand is
\mathbb{V}ar(u_{2,t+1})
). Then

\displaystyle\phi^{2}=\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}-\mathbb{E}(u_{2,t+1}^{2}))^{2}=\mathbb{V}ar(u_{2,t+1}^{2}-\sigma^{2}),

(4.8)

under conditional homoskedasticity, whose natural feasible counterpart is

\displaystyle\widehat{\phi}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)^{2}+q(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\phi}^{2}+q(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}),

(4.9)

where

\displaystyle q(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)\left((\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left((\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=q_{1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}),

(4.10)

where we can analyze both components separately. For instance, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the definition of
\widetilde{\phi}^{2}
we obtain

\displaystyle|q_{1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left((\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=2\widetilde{\phi}q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})^{1/2},

(4.11)

where
\widetilde{\phi}
is clearly consistent and hence bounded. This can be seen by using the definition of
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}
:

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left|\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-u_{t+1}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2T^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}|u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle+T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(4.12)

therefore,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\phi}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left(u_{t+1}^{2}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{2}+o_{p}(1)\to_{p}\phi.

(4.13)

Therefore, the key component to analyze is
q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})
. For this, the following expansion is the key:

\displaystyle\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}=2\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

(4.14)

as in the expansion (
4.3
). In combination,

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\omega}^{2}_{j}-\widehat{\omega}^{2}_{j}|=f_{j}(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j})|q(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})|\leq f_{j}(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j})[2\widetilde{\phi}q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})^{1/2}+q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})].

(4.15)

4.2
Interim Results

We notice that the expansions of the feasible statistics in (
4.2
) - (
4.6
) are essentially driven by

\displaystyle A=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

\displaystyle B=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}),

\displaystyle C=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}),

\displaystyle D=|\widetilde{\omega}^{2}_{j}-\widehat{\omega}^{2}_{j}|\quad\text{for}\quad j=1,\ldots,4,

which we write in the general notation. We will formulate our results in terms of
A,B,C
, which will immediately give
D
. Similarly to
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
and
Stauskas & Westerlund, (2022)
(in CCE setup),
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}
is the central component that we will use. Introduce
\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{I}_{k},\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r})\in\mathbb{R}^{(k+r)\times(k+r)}
, where
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}
is a rotation matrix for either homogeneous or heterogeneous cases defined in Section 3. Then

\displaystyle\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}

\displaystyle=y_{t+1}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}-(y_{t+1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t})=\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})+\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})+(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})+\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})+(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4},

(4.16)

where we used the local-to-alternative parameterization. The following lemma is the key in deriving the orders of
A,B
and
C
.

Lemma 10

Under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 with homogeneous
\alpha
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

Under heterogeneous
\alpha
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha_{r}})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

Proof.

\mathrm{(i.)}

It is convenient to utilize the stacked notation as used before. We additionally introduce
\mathbf{y}_{t}=(y_{2},\ldots,y_{t})^{\prime}
and
\mathbf{u}_{t}=(u_{2}\ldots,u_{t})^{\prime}
both in
\mathbb{R}^{t-1}
. Moreover, to keep the notation compact, we now let
\mathbf{F}_{t}=(\mathbf{f}_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,\mathbf{f}_{t-1}^{\prime})
, and
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t-1}^{\prime})
both in
\mathbb{R}^{t-1}
. We normalize
t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
for compactness, again. Implementation-wise, we can employ the restriction
(t-1)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, but then
t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\frac{t-1}{t}\frac{1}{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}=\frac{(t-1)/T}{t/T}\mathbf{I}_{r}=\mathbf{I}_{r}+O(T^{-1})
, and the conclusions would stay the same. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{t}=\mathbf{Z}_{t}\boldsymbol{\delta}+\mathbf{u}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}-(\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}_{t}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}+\mathbf{u}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}-(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}+\mathbf{u}_{t}

(4.17)

Further, by the FWL Theorem and the fact that for any conformable and invertible
\mathbf{A}
and
\mathbf{B}
matrices we have
\mathbf{B}^{-1}=\mathbf{A}^{\prime}(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{A}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{A}
, we obtain

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{I}_{k}&\mathbf{0}_{k\times r}\\
\mathbf{0}_{r\times k}&(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\\
\left(\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{I}_{k}&\mathbf{0}_{k\times r}\\
\mathbf{0}_{r\times k}&(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\\
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\\
\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\end{bmatrix},

(4.18)

which gives

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\begin{bmatrix}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{y}_{t}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\\
\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{y}_{t}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{y}_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{a}\\
\mathbf{b}\end{bmatrix}.

(4.19)

Clearly, since we have a vector,

\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}\right)\right\|^{2}=\left\|\mathbf{a}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{b}\right\|^{2}
, therefore, we will analyse the sub-vectors. By using (
4.17
), we can further decompose:

\displaystyle T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\begin{bmatrix}\left(\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}\\
\left(\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle-T^{-1/4}\begin{bmatrix}\left(\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\right)(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\\
\left(\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\right)(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\begin{bmatrix}\left(\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\right)\mathbf{u}_{t}\\
\left(\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}-\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\right)\mathbf{u}_{t}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{I}\\
\mathbf{II}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{III}\\
\mathbf{IV}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{V}\\
\mathbf{VI}\end{bmatrix},

(4.20)

where each of the terms can be split into two again. Starting from
\mathbf{I}
, we can simply it with

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\boldsymbol{\theta}+T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}

\displaystyle=T^{1/4}\boldsymbol{\theta}

while

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}_{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\boldsymbol{\theta}+\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}

\displaystyle=T^{1/4}\boldsymbol{\theta}+T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}

\displaystyle=T^{1/4}\boldsymbol{\theta}+T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}

\displaystyle-T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}

\displaystyle=T^{1/4}\boldsymbol{\theta}-\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},

(4.21)

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathbf{I}=\mathbf{I}_{1}-\mathbf{I}_{2}=\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}

(4.22)

and so

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{I}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})

(4.23)

by
Lemma 2
(ii). In particular, we used

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\left(\frac{1}{t}\mathbf{Z}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{t}\right)}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})\right).

(4.24)

Moving to the first component of
\mathbf{II}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}_{1}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\boldsymbol{\theta}+T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}

\displaystyle=T^{1/4}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}=(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}.

(4.25)

Next,

\displaystyle\mathbf{II}_{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}^{-1}_{Nt,r})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\delta}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\boldsymbol{\theta}

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}

\displaystyle-T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}(\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},

(4.26)

which follows the analysis in (
4.23
):

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{II}\right\|=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\||\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha}).

(4.27)

For
\mathbf{III}_{1}
, we note that by the PC estimation restriction we get

\displaystyle\mathbf{III}_{1}

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\left(\mathbf{W}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}

\displaystyle=\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}

\displaystyle+\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}

(4.28)

and so,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{III}_{1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1/2}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha}),

(4.29)

where we used the following facts. Firstly,
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\left(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right)}=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}(\mathbf{I}_{r})}=\sqrt{r}
. Next,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{s}\right\|\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{r}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}=O_{p}(1).

(4.30)

Finally, we need to verify that
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
. For this, we can use two decompositions similar to the ones in Lemma 2 of
Bai & Ng, (2002)
:

\displaystyle t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}})\mathbf{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}\mathbf{H}}-\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}})\mathbf{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=t^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}

\displaystyle+t^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}

\displaystyle+t^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}

\displaystyle+t^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\left[(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}-(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{-1}\right]\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{i+ii+iii+iv},

(4.31)

where
\mathbf{i}-\mathbf{iii}
are negligible based on Lemma
2
. For instance, by noting that
(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{i}\|\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}),

(4.32)

whereas

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{ii}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})

(4.33)

and
\|\mathbf{iii}\|
is of the same order. We are left with
\mathbf{iv}
, whose central component can be decomposed in a similar fashion:

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})

(4.34)

with the same holding for the inverses, which in connection to
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-2}\|\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\|^{2}=O_{p}(1)
means that
\|\mathbf{iv}\|
is of the same order. This discussion implies that
t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}=t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}+o_{p}(1)
uniformly in
t
and by the Continuous Mapping Theorem (CMT)

\displaystyle(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}=(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}+o_{p}(1)

(4.35)

uniformly in
t
. Clearly,

\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{III}_{2}\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})
,
because it has exactly the same form as (
4.23
):

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{III}_{2}\right\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha}),

(4.36)

and therefore, in total

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{III}\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha}).

(4.37)

Moving on to
\mathbf{IV}
, we get

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{IV}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\mathbf{W}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\|\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})

(4.38)

based on Lemma
2
and the results we used previously. Similarly to (
4.31
), we need to check whether
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
. Note how similarly to (
4.34
) we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-t^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1}\|\mathbf{W}_{t}\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle+2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|

\displaystyle+2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}-\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}t^{-1/2}\|\mathbf{W}_{t}\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\times\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})

(4.39)

and the same rate holds for the inverses. This discussion implies that

\displaystyle(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}=(t^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{\prime})^{-1}+o_{p}(1)

(4.40)

uniformly in
t
. By noting that
\mathbf{IV}_{2}
has exactly the same form as (
4.27
), we can deduct that overall

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{IV}\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha}).

(4.41)

For the remaining terms, we continue with
\mathbf{V}
and focus on
\mathbf{V}_{2}
first, to illustrate the approach. Because
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
by assumption, we can focus directly on its numerator. Particularly,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{f}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{f}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(4.42)

by Lemma 2.1 of
Corradi et al., (2001)
and
Tk_{0}^{-1}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\pi_{0}}}+O(T^{-1})
. The analysis of
\mathbf{V}_{1}
is similar. Because
\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(1)
by (
4.31
) and
(t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t})^{-1}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{\mathbf{F}}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(4.43)

because
\{\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\}_{s=1}^{t-1}
is still an MDS process, because
\mathbf{u}_{t}
is mean-independent from
(Nt)^{-1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t}^{\prime}
, and hence its eigenvectors. In particular,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\sum_{l=1}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}(u_{s+1}u_{l+1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbb{E}\left(u_{s+1}^{2}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}\right)=\frac{t}{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\sigma^{2}_{t+1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{t}{T}\mathbb{E}(\sigma^{2}_{t+1})\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\frac{t}{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=tT^{-1}\sigma^{2}r=s\sigma^{2}r+O(T^{-1})

(4.44)

for
s\in(\pi_{0},1]
, and this results holds uniformly in
t
. Again, we were able to split the expectation, because
\sigma_{t}^{2}
is independent of other model primitives, and hence of eigenvectors thereof. Therefore

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|^{2}\right)=O(1).

(4.45)

Thus,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{V}\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}).

(4.46)

Ultimately, due to (
4.39
), the numerator of
\mathbf{VI}_{2}
follows

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}t^{-1}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{f}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{f}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\|T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(4.47)

while the one of
\mathbf{VI}_{1}
follows

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}

\displaystyle\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|+\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}t^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\mathbf{W}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{T}{t}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{W}_{t})^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(4.48)

meaning that overall

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\|\mathbf{VI}\|=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}).

(4.49)

Combining the orders of
\mathbf{I}
-
\mathbf{VI}
, implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|=o_{p}(T^{-1/4}).

(4.50)

Because we will apply this result in sequences of Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities, we can combine the same terms
\mathbf{I}
-
\mathbf{VI}
again. Then by the CMT and
(\sum_{j=1}^{q}a_{j})^{2}\leq q\sum_{j=1}^{q}a_{j}^{2}
, we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(4.51)

which completes the proof.

\mathrm{(ii.)}
The proof is nearly identical to part
\mathrm{(i.)}
, because the only change comes from applying the rates from part (b) of Lemma
5
, Lemma
6
and Lemma
7
responsible for the heterogeneous weakness of factor loadings.

Lemma 11

Under Assumption A.1-A.4 with either homogeneous or heterogeneous loading weakness we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad A=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad B=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(iii.)}\quad C=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(iv.)}\quad D=|\widetilde{\omega}^{2}_{j}-\widehat{\omega}^{2}_{j}|=o_{p}(1)\quad\text{for}\quad j=1,\ldots,4.

We will conduct the proof by assuming homogeneous
\alpha\in(0,1]
. The heterogeneous case is almost identical, where we apply the appropriate rate lemmas.

Proof.

\mathrm{(i.)}
By using (
4.16
) and
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 3(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2})
we get

\displaystyle A=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left[(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left[(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=3(A1+A2+A3),

(4.52)

where
A_{3}
is straightforward:

\displaystyle A_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{Td_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|^{2}\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}).

(4.53)

Next,

\displaystyle A_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|^{2}T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{3/2}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}T^{-1/2}),

(4.54)

because

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{z}_{s}\mathbf{z}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{z}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|=O_{p}(1),

(4.55)

because by the MDS Functional Central Limit Theorem (FCLT) as
T\to\infty

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{z}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|\Rightarrow\sigma\sup_{s\in(\pi_{0},1)}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{z}}^{1/2}\mathbf{W}(s)\right\|,

(4.56)

where
\mathbf{B}(s)
is a standard Brownian Motion (see a similar argument in Lemma 2.1 of
Corradi et al.,, 2001
). Lastly,

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{T\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T}{d_{T}}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(4.57)

which is driven by the supremum component.
Because
k_{0}<T
in finite samples, we have that overall

\displaystyle|A|=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}),

(4.58)

which completes
\mathrm{(i.)}
.

We move to
\mathrm{(ii.)}
. We have

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(u_{t+1}+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{t}-(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t})(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{f}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=B1+B2-B3,

(4.59)

where we begin with
B2
and
B3
. In particular,

\displaystyle\left|B2\right|

\displaystyle=\left|T^{-1/4}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{f}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|T^{-1/4}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\||\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\left(\frac{T}{d_{T}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{T}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{-1/4}\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}}_{A}\right)^{1/2}

(4.60)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(4.61)

where
\frac{d_{T}}{T}=q_{2}-q_{1}+O(T^{-1})
, and a similar bound holds for
B3
:

\displaystyle|B3|\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\right\||\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\left(\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left(\frac{T}{d_{T}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}}_{A}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4}),

(4.62)

where

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{-1/4}\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{w}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{T}{t}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{w}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{w}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{f}_{s}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle+T^{-1/4}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}\mathbf{w}_{s}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1),

(4.63)

because by the MDS Functional Central Limit Theorem (FCLT) as
T\to\infty

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\mathbf{w}_{s}u_{s+1}\right\|\Rightarrow\sigma\sup_{s\in(\pi_{0},1)}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}}^{1/2}\mathbf{B}(s)\right\|,

(4.64)

where
\mathbf{B}(s)
is a standard Brownian Motion (see a similar argument in Lemma 2.1 of
Corradi et al.,, 2001
). Next is
B_{1}
, which follows a martingale structure and will require a more involved analysis. Particularly,

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})+\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}

\displaystyle=B_{11}+B_{12}+B_{13},

(4.65)

where

\displaystyle|B_{11}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}|u_{t+1}|\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})\right\|\left\|(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})\right\|\left\|(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})\right\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}|u_{t+1}|\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{-1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})\right\|\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}|u_{t+1}|\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u^{2}_{t+1}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha}),

(4.66)

because
\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})\right\|^{2}=\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})\right\|^{2}
. Next, we move to
A_{13}
. Let
\mathbf{H}_{r}
be the probability limit of
\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}
, and so

\displaystyle|B_{13}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13a}|+|B_{13b}|,

(4.67)

where by using the definition of
\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|B_{13a}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13aa}|+|B_{13ab}|+|B_{13ac}|+|B_{13ad}|.

(4.68)

Subsequently, we will invoke
A.4
in the further derivations. Recall that we impose
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\to c>0
as
(N,T)\to\infty
. By using this, we get that
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}=O(T^{-1/4})
. In addition, we will require that
N^{(1-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{1/4}=o(1)
. Clearly,
N^{(1-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{1/4}=\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}^{1/4}}\right)\frac{\sqrt{k_{0}}}{N^{\alpha}}=o(1)
if
\frac{\sqrt{k_{0}}}{N^{\alpha}}=o(1)
under our assumptions. Note that the latter restriction resembles
\sqrt{T}N^{-1}=o(1)
in
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
. It is stronger because of possibly weaker loadings, but both are identical under
\alpha=1
.

We will obtain the rates one-by-one. To begin with,

\displaystyle|B_{13aa}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13aai}|+|B_{13aaii}|,

(4.69)

where, by using

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{T}}

\displaystyle\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\right\||\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}\right)\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\right\|^{2}\times\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

we get
