\displaystyle|B_{13aaii}|

\displaystyle\leq d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right\||u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\gamma_{l,t}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)\times O_{p}(N^{(1-\alpha)}T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)\times O_{p}(N^{(1-\alpha)}T^{-1/4})=o_{p}(1),

(4.70)

since
N^{(1-\alpha)}T^{-1/4}=O(1)
by assumption. Note that the rate also comes from

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\gamma_{l,t}^{2}\leq\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\gamma_{l,t}^{2}=O(T^{-1})

(4.71)

and, based on the results in Lemma
6
and Lemma
7

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{l}+(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}-\mathbf{H}_{r})\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}+2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha}).

(4.72)

Up next,

\displaystyle B_{13aai}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left[\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right](\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=B_{13aai,\Lambda}+B_{13aai,D-\Lambda},

(4.73)

such that, by letting
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}=\mathbf{q}
and using the fact that
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{l,t,\alpha}
is non-random, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}ar(B_{13aai,\Lambda})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{s=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}u_{s+1}\mathbf{q}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{s}\sum_{j=1}^{s}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{j}\gamma_{j,s,\alpha}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma^{2}_{t+1}\left[\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}(\sigma^{2}_{t+1})\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sigma^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\left|\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}\right|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sigma^{2}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2})\gamma_{l,t,\alpha}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq l\leq T-1}\mathbb{E}(\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2})\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\gamma_{l,t}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{T}\right)=O(T^{-1}),

(4.74)

meaning that
|B_{13aai,\Lambda}|=O_{p}(T^{-1/2})
. Lastly, by using the same chain of inequalities as in (
4.70
),

\displaystyle|B_{13aai,D-\Lambda}|

\displaystyle\leq T^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\gamma^{2}_{l,t}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=T^{1/4}O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})

(4.75)

when
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\to c>0
. We move on to

\displaystyle|B_{13ab}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13abi}|+|B_{13abii}|,

(4.76)

where by two iterations of Cauchy-Schwarz inequality we get

\displaystyle|B_{13abii}|

\displaystyle=d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right\||u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\xi^{2}_{l,t}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)\times O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{d_{T}}{k_{0}}\right)^{1/4}O_{p}(k_{0}^{1/4})\times\left(O_{p}\left(N^{(3/2-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{-1/2}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)})\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(N^{(3/2-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{-1/4}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{1/4}),

(4.77)

which comes from

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\xi^{2}_{l,t}\leq\frac{T^{2}}{d_{T}k_{0}}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\xi_{l,t}^{2}=O_{p}(N^{-1}).

(4.78)

Hence, under
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\to c
and
\frac{\sqrt{T}}{N^{\alpha}}=o(1)
for
\alpha>0.5
, we have that

\displaystyle|B_{13abii}|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(N^{(3/2-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{-1/4}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)}k_{0}^{1/4})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{1/2-\alpha})+O_{p}(\sqrt{k_{0}}N^{-\alpha})=o_{p}(1).

(4.79)

Note that with Lemma
8
, we can make the latter less restrictive as we would require
\frac{T^{1/4}}{N^{(1-3\alpha)/2}}\to 0
. We further go to
B_{13abi}
, where, by letting
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}=\mathbf{q}
, we have

\displaystyle B_{13abi}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left[\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right](\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=B_{13abi,\Lambda}+B_{13abi,D-\Lambda}

(4.80)

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}ar\left(B_{13abi,\Lambda}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{s=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}u_{s+1}\mathbf{q}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{s}\sum_{j=1}^{s}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{j}\xi_{j,s,\alpha}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t+1}^{2}\left[\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t+1}^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t+1}^{2}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\left|\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}(\sigma_{t+1}^{2})\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{N^{4}}{N^{4\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(e_{i,l}e_{i,t}-\mathbb{E}(e_{i,l}e_{i,t}))\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\sigma^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{T}{d_{T}k_{0}}\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\left(\frac{N^{4}}{N^{4\alpha}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(e_{i,l}e_{i,t}-\mathbb{E}(e_{i,l}e_{i,t}))\right)^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O(T^{-1/2}N^{(1-2\alpha)}),

(4.81)

which means that

\displaystyle\left|B_{13abi,\Lambda}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}\right|=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})=o_{p}(1)

(4.82)

provided that
\alpha>0.5
. Also,

\displaystyle|B_{13abi,D-\Lambda}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left[\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right](\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}\right\|\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|T^{1/4}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\||u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}T^{1/4}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}T^{1/4}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\xi^{2}_{l,t}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)\times T^{1/4}\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)\times O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})

\displaystyle=O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})+O_{p}(\sqrt{k_{0}}N^{-\alpha})

(4.83)

under
\alpha>0.5
,
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\to c>0
and
\sqrt{T}N^{-\alpha}=o(1)
. Hence, overall,

\displaystyle|B_{13ab}|=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\xi_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|=o_{p}(1).

(4.84)

Next, we move to

\displaystyle|B_{13ac}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13aci}|+|B_{13acii}|,

(4.85)

where similarly to
B_{13abii}
:

\displaystyle|B_{13acii}|

\displaystyle\leq d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}\right\|\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right\||u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\eta^{2}_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)\times O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)

(4.86)

under
\alpha>0.5
,
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\to c>0
and
T^{1/4}N^{-\alpha/2}=\sqrt{T^{1/2}N^{-\alpha}}=o(1)
. Next, to obtain the sharpest possible rate,

\displaystyle|B_{13aci}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left[\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right](\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13aci,\Lambda}|+|B_{13aci,D-\Lambda}|

(4.87)

where

\displaystyle|B_{13aci,D-\Lambda}|=

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left[\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right](\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}T^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\eta^{2}_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)\times T^{1/4}\times O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(T^{1/4}N^{-\alpha/2})=o_{p}(1)

(4.88)

under the needed restrictions. Furthermore,

\displaystyle B_{13aci,\Lambda}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\mathbf{f}_{l}^{\prime}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\left[\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\mathbf{f}_{l}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right]\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=B_{13aci,\Lambda,FF-I}+B_{13aci,\Lambda,I}.

(4.89)

This, by letting again
\mathbf{q}=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}
, results in

\displaystyle|B_{13aci,\Lambda,FF-I}|

\displaystyle\leq d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\mathbf{f}_{l}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right\||u_{t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|d_{T}^{1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\mathbf{f}_{l}^{\prime}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}N^{-\alpha/2})

(4.90)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}ar(B_{13aci,\Lambda,I})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=O(T^{-1/2}N^{-\alpha}),

(4.91)

which implies that
|B_{13aci,\Lambda,I}|=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}N^{-\alpha/2})
. Therefore, overall,

\displaystyle|B_{13ac}|=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\eta_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|=o_{p}(1).

(4.92)

Eventually, we will adopt a similar strategy in case of

\displaystyle|B_{13ad}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l})\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13adi}|+|B_{13adii}|,

(4.93)

where

\displaystyle|B_{13adii}|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{l}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\nu^{2}_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(d_{T}^{1/4})\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)\times O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

(4.94)

as
\nu_{l,t,\alpha}
has the same behavior as
\eta_{l,t,\alpha}
in (
4.86
). Next,

\displaystyle|B_{13adi}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\left[\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right](\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13adi,\Lambda}|+|B_{13adi,D-\Lambda}|,

(4.95)

where by following the exact same steps in (
4.88
), we have

\displaystyle|B_{13adi,D-\Lambda}|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(1)\times T^{1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\mathbf{D}_{Nt,r}^{2}\right)^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}\right\|\times\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}k_{0}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\eta^{2}_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)\times T^{1/4}\times O_{p}(N^{-\alpha/2})\times\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(T^{1/4}N^{-\alpha/2})=o_{p}(1).

(4.96)

To obtain the sharp rate, notice how by definition of
\nu_{l,t,\alpha}

\displaystyle B_{13adi,\Lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{l}\nu_{l,t,\alpha}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,l}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1},

(4.97)

and so, by using the same chain of inequalities as in
Gonçalves et al., (2017)
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}ar(B_{13adi,\Lambda})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{H}_{r}\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{q}u_{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t+1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}(\sigma_{t+1}^{2})\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{q}\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\mathbf{f}_{l}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right\|^{4}\right)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left[\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{t}\sum_{l=1}^{t}\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{4}\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right\|^{4}\right)\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{T}{k_{0}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{l=1}^{T}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{f}_{l}\right\|^{8}\right)\right)^{1/4}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}e_{i,l}\right\|^{8}\right)\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle=O(T^{-1/2}N^{-\alpha}),

(4.98)

implying that

\displaystyle|B_{13adi,\Lambda}|=O_{p}(T^{-1/4}N^{-\alpha/2}).

(4.99)

Note that under independence of
\mathbf{f}_{t}
and the idiosynratics, we would need only the 4th moment. This analysis brings us back to (
4.67
), and therefore

\displaystyle|B_{13}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}T^{-1/4}\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{13a}|+|B_{13b}|=o_{p}(1),

(4.100)

because by Lemma
7
, we have

\displaystyle|B_{13b}|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(1)T^{1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}^{-1}-\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1}\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(1)T^{1/4}\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N^{2}}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1-2\alpha})\right)=o_{p}(1)

(4.101)

under
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{k_{0}^{1/4}}\to c>0
and
\sqrt{k_{0}}N^{-\alpha}=o(1)
.

We are left to analyze
B_{12}
, which will now follow based on the interim results developed before, Let
\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{I}_{k},\mathbf{H}_{r})
, then

\displaystyle|B_{12}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right|

\displaystyle=|B_{12,a}|+|B_{12,b}|,

(4.102)

where

\displaystyle|B_{12,b}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}|u_{t+1}|\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle=d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}|u_{t+1}|\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq d_{T}^{1/4}\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|\left(\frac{1}{d_{t}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{f}_{t}\right\|\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O(1)\times d_{T}^{1/4}\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})\right)\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})\right)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)

(4.103)

under
\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\to c>0
and
\sqrt{k_{0}}N^{-\alpha}=o(1)
. Also, by the existence of moments,

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}ar(B_{12,a})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\sum_{s=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}u_{s+1}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}^{\prime}T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{s}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Ns,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{s})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}\mathbf{z}_{s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t+1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\sigma_{t+1}^{4}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=(\mathbb{E}(\sigma_{t+1}^{4}))^{1/2}\left\|\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}\right\|^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{4}\right)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=o(T^{-1/2})

(4.104)

under the appropriate restrictions, which implies that
|B_{12,a}|=o_{p}(1)
. Note that here we needed to implement Cauchy-Schwarz inequality on
\sigma_{t+1}^{2}
, as well, because due to GARCH effects, it includes lags of
u_{t}^{2}
, which is not independent of
T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})
since it is a function of
u_{t-1}
and deeper lags. This completes the analysis of
B_{1}
, which means that overall

\displaystyle|B|\leq\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{f}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\theta})^{\prime}\mathbf{w}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|=o_{p}(1).

(4.105)

Next, we go to
\mathrm{(iii.)}
, where

\displaystyle C=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})}_{\text{$B_{1}=o_{p}(1)$}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=C_{1}-C_{2},

(4.106)

and

\displaystyle\left|C_{2}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\||\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/2}\frac{1}{d_{T}^{1/4}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|\left(\frac{d_{T}}{T}\right)^{1/4}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}}\left(\frac{1}{d_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}}_{A}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T^{1/4}\sqrt{k_{0}}}\right)+O_{p}(T^{-1/4}N^{(1-2\alpha)/2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/2}).

(4.107)

Lastly,
C_{1}=B_{1}
, which was shown to be negligible in
\mathrm{(ii.)}
. Therefore, overall

\displaystyle|C|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|=o_{p}(1)

(4.108)

as expected.

Ultimately, we analyze
\mathrm{(iv.)}
. By following the result in (
4.15
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\widetilde{\omega}^{2}_{j}-\widehat{\omega}^{2}_{j}|=f_{j}(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{j})|q(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})|

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\left(q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})^{1/2}+q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})\right)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

(4.109)

where we only need to analyze
q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})
. By using
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
, we get

\displaystyle q_{2}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left((\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})^{2}+\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}^{2})\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=2(q_{21}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})+q_{22}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1}))=o_{p}(1),

(4.110)

where, by inserting the expansion in (
4.14
), we get

\displaystyle q_{22}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})=\left(\frac{2}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

(4.111)

by the parts (i.) and (iii.) of this Lemma, while again by
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
and (
4.14
)

\displaystyle q_{21}(\widetilde{u}^{2}_{2,t+1},\widehat{u}^{2}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left[2\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}+2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}}_{o_{p}(1)}+2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}

\displaystyle=4\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}+o_{p}(n^{-1/2})=o_{p}(1).

(4.112)

The latter comes from the fact that
\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}
is consistent for
u_{t+1}^{2}
uniformly in
t
:

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}(y_{t+1}-\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}_{t}}^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}(u_{t+1}-(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})^{\prime}\mathbf{z}_{t})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}u_{t+1}^{2}+2T^{-1}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|^{2}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}u_{t+1}^{2}+O_{p}(T^{-1})=O_{p}(1),

(4.113)

and also, by using
(a+b+c)^{4}\leq 4(a^{4}+b^{4}+c^{4})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{1}{T^{2}n}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{4,Nt,r}\mathbf{z}_{t})\right\|^{4}\left\|(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{4,Nt,r}^{-1})\right\|^{4}\left\|\sqrt{T}(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\delta})\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle+4\frac{1}{Tn}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{z}}_{t}\right\|^{4}\left\|T^{1/4}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{4,Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle+4\frac{1}{Tn}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})\right\|^{4}\left\|(\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}^{-1})\right\|^{4}\left\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right\|^{4}=o_{p}(1),

(4.114)

where subscript
4
indicates that we chose the fourth asymptotically equivalent rotation matrix described in Lemma 3 in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
, such that
\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}=(\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}
. Therefore, the first three terms in (
4.114
) are negligible based on the previous results, while the equivalent rotation matrix allows to write

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}\right)^{-1}(\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}e_{i,t}\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle+4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\frac{1}{N^{\alpha}}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{H}_{4,Nt,r}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|^{4}+O_{p}(N^{-2\alpha})

\displaystyle\leq\left(O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{T}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{N}}{N^{3\alpha/2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{2\alpha}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right)^{4}=o_{p}(1)

(4.115)

based on (18) on p. 1901 in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
and our assumptions, where
A.3
plays the central role. Similarly, under heterogeneous weakness, by using the usual rotation matrix,

\displaystyle\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{t}-\mathbf{H}_{Nt,r}\mathbf{f}_{t})\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}^{2}_{Nt,r})^{-1}(\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{B}_{N})\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle+4\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}^{2}_{Nt,r})^{-1}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|^{4}

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\right\|^{4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|(N\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-2}\mathbf{D}^{2}_{Nt,r})^{-1}\right\|^{4}\sup_{k_{0}\leq t\leq T-1}\left\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|^{4}+O_{p}(N^{-2\alpha_{r}})

\displaystyle\leq\left(O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\times O_{p}\left(\frac{N}{N^{\alpha_{r}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}(N^{1/2-3\alpha_{r}/2})\right)^{4}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)

(4.116)

based on (27) on p. 1905 in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
and our assumptions.

Remark 7

It is important to comment on the way the proof proceeds in case
\{u_{t}\}
is arbitrarily serially correlated. To illustrate the logic, we turn to the term
B_{12,a}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}_{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}u_{t+1}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{r}^{\prime}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{\Phi}_{Nt,r}^{-1})^{\prime}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\delta}}_{t})
, which in turn can be decomposed into several parts along the decomposition in (
4.20
). In particular, we focus on
\mathbf{V}
and
\mathbf{V}_{2}
specifically, as the argument for the rest of the terms will be identical. Once applied to
\mathbf{V}_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle B_{12,a}(\mathbf{V}_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left[\mathbf{w}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}\right]^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}^{\prime}\frac{T}{t}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}u_{t+1}}_{I}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\left[\mathbf{w}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}\right]^{\prime}\frac{T}{t}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}u_{t+1}}_{II}.

Let
t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}.\mathbf{f}}+o_{p}(1)
. The second term then

\displaystyle\sqrt{d_{T}}II

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\frac{T}{t}\left[\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right]^{\prime}T^{-1/2}\left[\mathbf{w}_{t}-\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}\right]u_{t+1}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\int_{s=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}s^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}.\mathbf{f}}^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}(s)\right)^{\prime}d\mathbf{W}(s)+b,

(4.117)

where
\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}=\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{w}_{t}\right)
,
b
is a bias term, and
\mathbf{B}(s)
and
\mathbf{W}_{s}
represent Brownian motions. This convergence will be satisfied if
\{\mathbf{z}_{t},u_{t+1}\}_{t=1}^{T}
is a mixing process with a certain size (see
Hansen,, 1992
). Therefore,
|II|=O_{p}(d_{T}^{-1/2})
. The similar argument applies to
I
:

\displaystyle\sqrt{d_{T}}I

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}^{\prime}\frac{T}{t}\left(t^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}T^{-1/2}u_{t+1}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\boldsymbol{\mu}_{\mathbf{w}}^{\prime}\int_{s=q_{1}}^{q_{2}}s^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}.\mathbf{f}}^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}(s)dv(s)+c

(4.118)

,
as
T\to\infty
, where
v(s)
is another (scalar) Brownian motion. In total, we then have that

\displaystyle\left|B_{12,a}(\mathbf{V}_{2})\right|=\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\mathbf{w}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\mathbf{F}}\mathbf{u}_{t}u_{t+1}\right|=O_{p}(d_{T}^{-1/2}).

(4.119)

Similar arguments, where and MDS assumption is altered to a high-level condition, can be applied to other terms in Lemma 7.

4.3
Main Results

Theorem 2

Under Assumption A.1 - A.4 with either homogeneous or heterogeneous loading weakness we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{(i.)}\quad g_{\widehat{f},1}=g_{f,1}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(ii.)}\quad g_{\widehat{f},2}=g_{f,2}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(iii.)}\quad g_{\widehat{f},3}=g_{f,3}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\mathrm{(iv.)}\quad g_{\widehat{f},4}=g_{f,4}+o_{p}(1).

Proof
.
\mathrm{(i.)}
. We apply Lemma
11
(iv.) and define
d_{T}=(k_{0}+m_{0}-1-k_{0}+1)=m_{0}
, such that
\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}=\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{0}(1-\pi_{0})}{1-\pi_{0}}}+O(T^{-1/2})=\sqrt{\mu_{0}}+O(T^{-1/2})
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+m_{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1).

(4.120)

Next, we let
d_{T}=(T-1-k_{0}-m_{0}+1)=T-k_{0}-m_{0}=n-m_{0}
, such that
\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}=\sqrt{1-\frac{m_{0}}{n}}=\sqrt{1-\mu_{0}}+O(T^{-1/2})
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}+m_{0}}^{T-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1).

(4.121)

\mathrm{(ii.)}
By application of Lemma
11
(iv.) and by defining
d_{T}=k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1-k_{0}+1=l_{2}^{0}
, such that
\sqrt{\frac{l_{2}^{0}}{n}}=\sqrt{\lambda_{2}^{0}}+O(n^{-1/2})
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})=o_{p}(1),

(4.122)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T)\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=o_{p}(1).

(4.123)

(\mathrm{iii.})
Follows by the exact same steps as
\mathrm{(ii.)}
, because averaging occurs only over
l_{1}
while holding
l_{2}^{0}
fixed.

\mathrm{(iv.)}
Again, we apply Lemma
11

\mathrm{(iv.)}
and define
d_{T}=k_{0}+l_{2}-1-k_{0}+1=l_{2}
, such that
\sqrt{\frac{l_{2}}{n}}=\sqrt{\lambda_{2}}+O(n^{-1/2})
. Then, given that
\sup_{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1\leq l_{2}\leq n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}=\frac{n}{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}=\tau_{0}^{-1}+O(n^{-1})
provided that
\tau_{0}\neq 0
and making use of the fact that
f_{2}(T)
also depends on
l_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\left(\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})-\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1\leq l_{2}\leq n}\left|\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T,l_{2})\rfloor}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}_{4}(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{l_{2}}{n}

\displaystyle+\sup_{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1\leq l_{2}\leq n}\left|\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T,l_{2})\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right|\frac{2}{\widehat{\omega}_{4}(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

(4.124)

because

\displaystyle\sup_{\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1\leq l_{2}\leq n}\left|\sqrt{\frac{d_{T}}{n}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{T}}}\sum_{t=\lfloor f_{1}(T)\rfloor}^{\lfloor f_{2}(T,l_{2})\rfloor}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\times\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{0}}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

(4.125)

while the supremum in the first term is negligible, since in the
g_{\widehat{f},2}
we demonstrated that such terms are negligible uniformly in
l_{2}
. More generally, they both follow directly based on the logic in
Pitarakis, (2025)
, and in particular, the passage from (A.6) to (A.8), which demonstrates that the remainder still vanishes inside of the integral. Lastly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{l_{2}}{n}=\int_{s=\tau_{0}}^{1}s^{-1}ds+o(1)=O(1).

(4.126)

4.4
Equivalence of the Power Enhancement

The statistics
g_{f,2}
,

g_{f,3}
and

g_{f,4}
can be power-enhanced, as argued in
Pitarakis, (2025)
. In particular, the choice of the infeasible adjustment terms are created by defining
\breve{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}=\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}
(see (33) in
Pitarakis,, 2025
). It is equivalent to adding the following terms to the respective statistics:

\displaystyle\widetilde{\zeta}(\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}_{2}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

(4.127)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\psi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}}_{3}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

(4.128)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\phi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

(4.129)

where the dependence on
\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0}
follows through the variance estimators.
23
23

23

Note that strictly we have
\widetilde{\psi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{1}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}
. However,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\psi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{1}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{n-\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}=\frac{1}{\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}+O(n^{-1}),

therefore they are equivalent.
Clearly, the feasible versions based on the estimated factors are given by

\displaystyle\widehat{\zeta}(\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}_{2}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

(4.130)

\displaystyle\widehat{\psi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{3}\frac{1}{\lambda_{2}^{0}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2},

(4.131)

\displaystyle\widehat{\phi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\frac{1}{\widehat{\omega}}_{4}\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

(4.132)

We can approximate them with the feasible adjustments.

Proposition 5

Under Assumptions A.1 - A.4 with either homogeneous or heterogeneous loading weakness we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\zeta}(\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\widetilde{\zeta}(\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\widehat{\psi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\widetilde{\psi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\widehat{\phi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})=\widetilde{\phi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})+o_{p}(1).

Proof
. Given part
\mathrm{(iv.)}
of Lemma
11
, we can demonstrate that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}+\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}+o_{p}(1),

(4.133)

because the last term is negligible based on Lemma
11
(i.). Moreover, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}

\displaystyle-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\Bigg|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}|\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}||\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1}|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

(4.134)

due to Lemma
11
, and the first component coming from the infeasible adjustment term, which is well-behaved under the local alternative. In particular,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}^{2}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}^{2}-u_{t+1}^{2})-\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-u_{t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}^{2}-u_{t+1}^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}^{0}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}^{2}-u_{t+1}^{2})+O_{p}(T^{-1/4})

\displaystyle\to_{p}\sqrt{1-\pi_{0}}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0\prime}(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{f}}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}_{\mathbf{w}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{f}})\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},

(4.135)

as
T\to\infty
, because of the arguments in the proof of Proposition
4
. The proof regarding the
\widehat{\phi}(\tau_{0},\lambda_{1}^{0},\lambda_{2}^{0})
follows the steps in (
2.51
), and it goes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}+o_{p}(1)

(4.136)

under our conditions, because the last term is exactly (
4.125
) and the middle term follows

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\times\underbrace{\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{k_{0}+l_{2}-1}(\widetilde{u}_{1,t+1}-\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}}_{O_{p}(1)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\times\left(\sqrt{1-\pi_{0}}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0\prime}(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{f}}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}_{\mathbf{w}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{f}})\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{n(1-\tau_{0})}\sum_{l_{2}=\lfloor n\tau_{0}\rfloor+1}^{n}\frac{n}{l_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=k_{0}}^{T-1}(\widetilde{u}_{2,t+1}-\widehat{u}_{2,t+1})^{2}\right)^{1/2}\times\left(\sqrt{1-\pi_{0}}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0\prime}(\mathbf{I}_{r}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{f}}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}_{\mathbf{w}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{w}\mathbf{f}})\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\tau_{0})}\int_{s=\tau_{0}}^{1}s^{-1}ds\right)

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

(4.137)

Appendix 5
Additional Monte Carlo Results

5.1
Design, Size and Power

We design a DGP similar to DGP2 in
Pitarakis, (2025)
but where factors are specified in the same way as DGP2 of
Bai & Ng, (2023)
with strong/ weak homogeneous/ heterogeneous loadings. Throughout, we set the number of factors
r=3
.

\displaystyle y_{t+1}=c+\theta_{1}y_{t}+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{t}+u_{t+1},\quad\mathbf{f}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{r=3},

\displaystyle u_{t+1}=\begin{cases}NID(0,1)\hskip 5.69054pt(\textit{Baseline}),\\
\sigma_{t+1}\varepsilon_{t+1},\quad\varepsilon_{t+1}\sim NID(0,1),\quad\sigma^{2}_{t+1}=\omega+\alpha u_{t}^{2}+\eta\sigma^{2}_{t},\quad\omega,\alpha=0.1,\quad\eta=0.2,\end{cases}

\displaystyle x_{i,t}=\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{t}+e_{i,t},

\displaystyle e_{i,t}=\rho_{i}e_{i,t-1}+\sqrt{1-\rho_{i}^{2}}v_{i,t},\quad v_{i,t}=\begin{cases}NID(0,1)\hskip 5.69054pt(\textit{Baseline}),\\
\epsilon_{i,t}+\sum_{k=i+1}^{K}\xi(\epsilon_{i-k,t}+\epsilon_{i+k,t}),\quad\epsilon_{i,0}=0,\quad K=5,\quad\xi=0.4,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\mathbf{f}_{t}\sim N(0,\mathbf{I}_{3}),\quad\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}\sim\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{B}_{N}/\sqrt{N}+\mathbf{G}_{i}\pi/\sqrt{N},\quad\mathbf{F}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}/T\approx\mathbf{I}_{3},\quad\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\approx\mathbf{D}^{2},

where:
\mathbf{G}_{i}\sim N(0,\mathbf{I}_{3})
,
\mathbf{D}^{2}=\operatorname{diag}(3\;2\;1);

\mathbf{B}_{N}=\operatorname{diag}(N^{\alpha_{1}/2}\;N^{\alpha_{2}/2}\;N^{\alpha_{3}/2})
,
(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3})=(1,1,1)
for strong homogeneous loadings,
(0.51,0.51,0.51)
for weak homogeneous loadings,
(1,0.7,0.51)
for mixed strong/weak heterogeneous loadings.

Remark 8

Note that
\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}
is simulated differently than in
Bai & Ng, (2023)
. The reason is that the rate of
\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}\|
plays an important role in our asymptotic analysis, whereas it did not matter for
Bai & Ng, (2023)
. This simulation method mimics A.3 i), because we can show that under such design
\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}=\mathbf{D}^{2}+O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2})
as desired. Consider
\boldsymbol{\lambda}_{i}\sim\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{B}_{N}/\sqrt{N}+\mathbf{G}_{i}\pi/\sqrt{N}
, where
\pi>0
regulates the strength of the random perturbation:

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{D}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\mathbf{D}\mathbf{B}_{N}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{D}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\right)\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{B}_{N}\mathbf{D}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{D}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\mathbf{D}+\mathbf{D}\pi\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}+\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{D}\pi\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\pi^{2}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}=1+2+3+4.

Clearly, 1 is
O_{p}(1)
. Next, 2 (and hence 3) is
O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2})
:

\displaystyle\left\|2\right\|=\left\|3\right\|\leq\pi\left\|\mathbf{D}\right\|\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{B}^{-1}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}/2}).

Lastly,

\displaystyle\|4\|\leq\pi^{2}\|\mathbf{B}_{N}^{-1}\|^{2}\left\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{G}_{i}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}_{i}\right\|=O_{p}(N^{-\alpha_{r}}).

Therefore, the perturbation
\mathbf{G}_{i}\pi/\sqrt{N}
induces the reaction to the weakest loading. If the factors are strong, we obtain
O_{p}(N^{-1/2})
rate as it should be. Of course, the functional form of the perturbation can be altered, as
\mathbf{G}_{i}
can be substituted for another random matrix, which may be correlated or uncorrelated to
\mathbf{G}_{i}
.

Cross-sectional and time dimensions are as follows:
(N,T)=(800,500)
. For the practical implementations, following
Pitarakis, (2025)
, we set
c=1.25
,
\theta_{1}=0.5
,
\rho_{i}=0.3+N(0,1)_{i}\times 0.5
,
\boldsymbol{\beta}=(0,0,0)^{\prime}
,
\pi=24
, for size, and
\boldsymbol{\beta}=(j,j,j)^{\prime}
for
j\in\{0.1,0.2,0.3,0.35,0.4,\allowbreak 0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6\}
for power.

5.4

forecast errors (see Section 3.3 of the main paper), when the loadings are both homogeneous and heterogeneous.
